Optimization algorithm details:
- Read parent `height_sequence_1`. Normalize to p0 = a/sum (clip nonnegative). n=6962. If not available, use base shapes.
- Candidate storage: `best_list`, `best_score` exact `evaluate_sequence`.
- Precompute FFT sizes: N2=nextpow2(2n-1)=16384; N3=nextpow2(3n-2)=32768.
- Define `pp_conv(p)`: fa=rfft(p,N2); c=irfft(fa*fa,N2)[:2n-1]. This returns full convolution coefficients. Sum c =1 (should).
- Define `grad_from_r(p, q)`: q length 2n-1, weights sum 1 or arbitrary? For gradient of ∑ q_k C_k. If q sum mass, gradient d=2*corr(q,p_rev). Compute fq=rfft(q,N3), fr=rfft(p[::-1],N3), c=irfft(fq*fr,N3); d=2*c[n-1:n-1+n]. If q normalized? yes. For logsumexp, q normalized sum1, gradient of F=∑q C: g=d. But if F=logsumexp, derivative dF=∑q dC = d. No 1/T because q normalized. Good. Objective value `F = Cmax + logsum/T`? Actually logsumexp(T C)=Cmax + (1/T)log ∑ exp(T(C_i-Cmax)). Define fast functions:
```
N2 = _p2(2*n-1)
def conv_coef(p):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(p,N2)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N2)[:2*n-1]
def score_from_conv(C): return 2*n*C.max()
```
For p sum1, C sum=1. For arrays not exactly sum1 due normalization? We'll normalize p before.

Gradient:
```
N3 = _p2(3*n-2)
p_rev = p[::-1]
def pfft(p, size=N2): return np.fft.rfft(p, size)
def grad_C(p, q):
    fr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N3)
    fq = np.fft.rfft(q, N3)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fq*fr, N3)
    return 2.0 * c[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check index length: `c` length N3; take slice n-1:n-1+n (end 2n-1). Earlier index r+n-1 for r=0..n-1. Good.

Objective function:
```
state = {'T': T, 'best_Cmax': None, 'p': None}
def _obj(y):
    y=np.asarray(y)
    if not finite: return large, None
    p=softmax(y)
    C=conv_coef(p)
    Cmax=C.max()
    if not finite: return inf
    z=T*(C-Cmax)
    # sum exp; if T very high and many -100, exp underflow zero; okay, if all zero due Cmax? at least Cmax one exp0 =>Z>=1
    ex=np.exp(np.clip(z, -745, 0))
    Z=ex.sum()
    val = 2.0*n*Cmax + (2.0*n)/T * math.log(Z)  # constant 2n; logZ<=0
    # for gradient, q=ex/Z
    q=ex/Z
    g=grad_C(p,q)
    mean = np.dot(p,g)
    grad_y = p*(g - mean)
    # scale? val and grad consistent if val includes 2n.
    state['Cmax']=Cmax; state['p']=p
    return val, grad_y
```

```
ridge=1e-9
def fun(y):
    y=np.asarray(y)
    if not finite: return 1e6, None
    p=softmax(y)
    C=conv(p)
    Cmax=C.max()
    if not finite: return 1e6, np.zeros_like(y)
    z=T*(C-Cmax)
    ex=np.exp(z) # z<=0
    Z=ex.sum()
    if Z<=0: ...
    val=2.0*n*Cmax + (2.0*n)/T * math.log(Z)
    val += ridge*np.dot(y-y.mean(), y-y.mean())
    q=ex/Z
    g=grad(p,q)
    grad_y=2.0*n*p*(g-np.dot(p,g))
    grad_y += 2*ridge*(y-y.mean())
    state['last_Cmax']=Cmax
    state['last_p']=p
    return val, grad_y
```
Since `fun` may be called many times, avoid allocate large? Arrays okay.

Active polish details:
```
def exact_score_p(p, C=None):
   if C is None: C=conv(p)
   return 2*n*C.max(), C

def polish_active(p, seconds, Tfactor=30.0):
   p=p/sum
   y=np.log(p+1e-300)
   y-=np.mean(y)
   start=time
   last_sc=score_p(p)[0]
   for it in range(500):
      if time>start+seconds: break
      C=conv(p); M=C.max(); sc=2*n*M; update best
      if M<=0: break
      T = Tfactor / M  # maybe 30
      z=T*(C-M); ex=np.exp(np.clip(z,-700,0)); q=ex/ex.sum()
      g=grad_C(p,q)
      h = g - np.dot(p,g)  # unweighted mean? To remove constant? For y step, use h? g includes factor 2. For multiplicative p_new, step y-= eta*h maybe.
      maxh=np.max(np.abs(h))
      if maxh <= 1e-16: break
      # Try step sizes: eta = scale / maxh? The y change = eta*h. If eta in [1e-3, 100]
      y0 = np.log(p+1e-300) # p positive
      best_local_sc=sc; best_local_p=p
      # To avoid too many evals, candidates: factors around 0.1
      scales = np.array([0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0,30.0,100.0]) / maxh
      # Also negative? Gradient descent direction should positive; maybe try - for rare? no.
      for s in scales:
          p_new=softmax(y0 - s*h)
          Cn=conv(p_new); scn=2*n*Cn.max()
          if scn < best_local_sc*(1-1e-10): ...
      if not improved:
          # maybe try smaller:
          for s in np.logspace(-5,-3,6)/maxh:
             ...
          if not improved: break
      p=best_local_p; last_sc=best_local_sc
   update best
   return p
```

Softmax function:
```
def softmax(y):
    y=np.asarray(y,dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
       return None
    ym=y-y.max()
    # Clip for exp: lower -745, upper 0
    e=np.exp(np.clip(ym, -745.0, 709.0)) # ym max 0, no >0
    s=e.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not finite: return None
    return e/s
```
Since ym max0, upper not needed, but if max -inf? If all -inf, return uniform? We'll check finite y before.

In L-BFGS, y can be >700? Bounds prevent. In line search polish, y may be outside; softmax clips. For line search, after `y0 - s*h`, values can be less than -745; exp underflow zero; if all underflow except maybe, okay. If all zero due max less than -745? The max after clipping? `ym=y-y.max()`, if y.max() finite, max ym=0, exp0=1. Good.

Code for `run_lfbfgs` in detail:
```
def run_lfbfgs(p_start, T, seconds):
    p=np.asarray(p_start,float); s=p.sum(); p=p/s
    y = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    y = y - np.mean(y)
    y = np.clip(y, -30.0, 30.0)
    end = min(hard_deadline, time.monotonic()+seconds)
    state={}
    ridge=1e-9
    def fun(y):
        nonlocal best_p,best_score? Use list.
        now=time.monotonic()
        if now > end:
            stop[0]=True
            # return large grad zero
            return 1e6, np.zeros_like(safe_y)
        p=softmax(y)
        if p is None: return 1e6, np.zeros_like(y)
        C=conv(p)
        sc=2*n*C.max()
        if sc < best[1]: best[1]=sc; best[0]=p.copy()
        if not np.isfinite(sc): return 1e6, np.zeros_like(y)
        Cmax=sc/(2*n)
        z=T*(C-Cmax)
        ex=np.exp(np.clip(z,-745,0))
        Z=ex.sum()
        if Z<=1e-300:
           # uniform over max
           q=np.zeros(2*n-1); q[C==Cmax]=1/(C==Cmax).sum()
           val=sc
        else:
           q=ex/Z
           val=sc + 2*n/T*math.log(Z)
        # q sum maybe 1
        g=grad_C(p,q)
        mu=float(np.dot(p,g))
        grad_y=2*n*p*(g-mu)
        yc=y-y.mean()
        val += ridge*np.dot(yc,yc)
        grad_y += 2*ridge*yc
        # If grad not finite
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_y)):
            grad_y=np.zeros_like(y)
            val=1e6
        return val, grad_y
    stop=[False]
    def cb(x):
       if time.monotonic() > end:
          stop[0]=True
          return True
       return False
    bounds=[(-30.0,30.0)] for _ in range(n)]
    try:
       res=minimize(fun, y, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, callback=cb,
                    options={'maxiter':300, 'maxfun':3000, 'maxls':20, 'pgtol':1e-14, 'ftol':1e-12, 'maxcorr':50, 'disp':False})
    except Exception:
       pass
    # final from res? not needed best updated
    return best[0].copy()
```

Adam code with T factor:
```
def adam_search(a0, seconds, factors):
   a=a0.copy(); if sum bad a=ones*n
   m=v=zeros; step=0
   lr=0.02
   for fac in factors:
       end=min(hard, time+seconds/fac_count?)
       while time<end:
          S=a.sum()
          if S<=0: a=ones
          p=a/S
          C=conv(p); Cmax=C.max(); if not finite break
          sc=2n*Cmax; update best from p
          if Cmax<=1e-300: break
          T=fac / Cmax
          z=T*(C-Cmax); ex=np.exp(np.clip(z,-745,0)); Z=ex.sum()
          if Z<=0: q uniform
          q=ex/Z
          M_p=Cmax + math.log(Z)/T
          d=grad_C(p,q)
          grad = 2.0*n*d - (4.0*n*M_p)/S
          # Adam
          step+=1
          m=0.9m+0.1grad; v=0.999v+0.001grad^2
          mb=m/(1-0.9**step); vb=v/(1-0.999**step)
          lr = lr0 * (0.5 ** (stage_idx/2)) maybe
          a_new = a - lr*mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+1e-8)
          a_new=np.clip(a_new,0,1000)
          if a_new.sum() <=0: a_new=a.copy()
          a_new *= n/a_new.sum()
          # Reset momentum if bad? score maybe.
          a=a_new
       # after stage, maybe symmetrize
```

Let's code `adam_search` as a function using global `best`. It should return `best_p` updated.

```
def adam_search(a0, seconds, factors):
    # make a0
    a=np.array(a0,float); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: a=np.ones(n); else a*= n/s
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    a*=n/a.sum()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); step=0
    last_improve = time.monotonic()
    stage_end = time.monotonic()+seconds/len(factors) maybe
    for fac in factors:
       end = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+seg)
       lr0 = 0.025 * (0.75 ** stage_idx)
       while time.monotonic() < end:
          S=a.sum()
          if S<=0: a=best_p*n
          p=a/S
          C=conv(p); Cmax=C.max(); sc=2n*Cmax
          if sc < best[1]: best... last_improve=now
          if Cmax<=1e-300: break
          T=fac / Cmax
          z=T*(C-Cmax); ex=np.exp(np.clip(z,-745,0)); Z=ex.sum()
          if Z<=1e-300: q=np.full(2n-1,1/(2n-1)) # or active; but at least ex max1 so Z ok
          q=ex/Z
          M = Cmax + math.log(Z)/T
          d=grad_C(p,q)
          grad = 2*n*d - (4*n*M)/S
          if not finite: break
          step +=1
          m=...
          lr=lr0*(0.999 ** (step%?)) maybe decay with stage iteration: keep `stage_iter`.
          a_new = a - lr * mb/(sqrt(vb)+1e-8)
          a_new = np.clip(a_new,0,1000)
          s2=a_new.sum()
          if s2<=0: a_new=a
          else: a_new *= n/s2
          # Optional sanity: if score of a_new (with conv) is much worse than best by > factor? not check.
          a=a_new
       # after stage, maybe if no improve ...
    # final normalize best? update from a
   p=a/a.sum(); sc=...
   update best
```

    if C is None: C=conv(p)

```python
import numpy as np
import math
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    _HAS_SCIPY_MIN = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY_MIN = False


def _p2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << ((m - 1).bit_length())


def _softmax(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if y.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        return None
    ymax = float(np.max(y))
    if not np.isfinite(ymax):
        return None
    z = y - ymax
    with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore"):
        e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return e / s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s)) - 35.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Parent construction, if present.
    base = None
    try:
        b = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if b.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
            base = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        base = None

    if base is None or float(np.sum(base)) <= 1e-12:
        n_default = 6962
        base = np.linspace(1e-3, 1.0, n_default, dtype=np.float64)

    n = int(len(base))
    if n <= 0:
        n = 6962
        base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    base = np.clip(base, 0.0, 1000.0)
    sbase = float(np.sum(base))
    if not np.isfinite(sbase) or sbase <= 1e-12:
        base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        sbase = float(n)

    p0 = base / sbase
    p0 = np.clip(p0, 0.0, None)

    N2 = _p2(2 * n - 1)
    N3 = _p2(3 * n - 2)

    def conv_self(p):
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(p, N2)
        out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N2)
        return out[: 2 * n - 1]

    def grad_quadratic(p, q):
        # Gradient of sum_k q_k * (p*p)_k with respect to p.
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
        q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
        pq = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N3)
        fq = np.fft.rfft(q, N3)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fq * pq, N3)
        return 2.0 * c[n - 1: n - 1 + n]

    def normalized_p(x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if x.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return None
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1e6)
        s = float(np.sum(x))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return None
        return x / s

    def normalize_a(a):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if a.size != n:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.clip(a, 1e-9, 1000.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if s <= 1e-12:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(n)
        return a * (float(n) / s)

    best = [p0.copy(), float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(conv_self(p0))))]

    def update_best_from_p(p, C=None):
        p = normalized_p(p)
        if p is None:
            return
        if C is None:
            C = conv_self(p)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * np.max(C))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best[1]:
            best[1] = sc
            best[0] = p.copy()

    def add_candidate(p):
        update_best_from_p(p)

    # Killed by our own deadline.
    class _Timeout(Exception):
        pass

    def time_ok(slack=0.0):
        return time.monotonic() < deadline - slack

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Quick legitimate variants around the parent.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time_ok(2.0):
        add_candidate(p0)
        add_candidate((p0 + p0[::-1]) * 0.5)

        # Low-pass smoothing.
        for r in (2, 4, 8):
            w = np.abs(np.arange(-r, r + 1, dtype=np.float64))
            w *= w
            w /= float(np.sum(w))
            q = np.convolve(p0, w, mode="same")
            add_candidate(q)

        # Small support noise to avoid exact zero stalling.
        for eps in (1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2):
            add_candidate(p0 + eps * rng.random(n))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Adam-style damped gradient search on scale-normalized a.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def adam_search(a0, seconds, factors):
        a = normalize_a(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1))
        m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        beta1, beta2 = 0.90, 0.999
        global_step = 0

        nf = max(1, len(factors))
        seg = max(1.5, float(seconds) / float(nf))

        for idx, factor in enumerate(factors):
            if not time_ok(2.0):
                break
            stage_end = min(deadline - 2.0, time.monotonic() + seg)
            lr0 = 0.032 * (0.78 ** idx)
            stage_step = 0

            while time.monotonic() < stage_end and time_ok(1.0):
                s = float(np.sum(a))
                if s <= 1e-12:
                    a = best[0].copy() * float(n)
                    s = float(n)
                p = a / s
                C = conv_self(p)
                cmax = float(np.max(C))
                if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
                    break
                sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
                if sc < best[1]:
                    best[1] = sc
                    best[0] = p.copy()

                T = float(factor) / cmax
                z = T * (C - cmax)
                with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore"):
                    ex = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
                Z = float(np.sum(ex))
                if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 1e-300:
                    q = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / (2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
                else:
                    q = ex / Z

                M = cmax + math.log(Z) / T
                d = grad_quadratic(p, q)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
                    break

                grad = 2.0 * float(n) * d - (4.0 * float(n) * M) / s
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
                    break

                global_step += 1
                stage_step += 1
                m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
                v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (grad * grad)
                mb = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** global_step)
                vb = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** global_step)
                lr = lr0 / (1.0 + 0.00012 * stage_step)

                a_new = a - lr * mb / (np.sqrt(vb) + 1e-8)
                a_new = np.clip(a_new, 1e-9, 1000.0)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(a_new)):
                    a_new = a.copy()
                a = normalize_a(a_new)

                # Occasional symmetrization probe (cheap in terms of search space).
                if stage_step and stage_step % 5000 == 0:
                    qsym = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
                    update_best_from_p(qsym)

        p = a / max(1e-300, float(np.sum(a)))
        update_best_from_p(p)

    if time_ok(10.0):
        t_rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if _HAS_SCIPY_MIN:
            t_adam = 0.58 * t_rem
            t_lbgfgs = 0.22 * t_rem
            t_polish = 0.18 * t_rem
        else:
            t_adam = 0.78 * t_rem
            t_lbgfgs = 0.0
            t_polish = 0.22 * t_rem

        if t_adam > 15.0:
            adam_search(best[0].copy() * float(n), t_adam,
                        factors=[3.0, 6.0, 12.0, 24.0, 48.0])

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Local L-BFGS-B polishing in log-simplex coordinates.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        if _HAS_SCIPY_MIN and time_ok(60.0) and t_lbgfgs > 45.0:
            bounds = [(-30.0, 30.0)] * n

            def run_lbgfgs(p_start, seconds, T_factor):
                p_start = normalized_p(p_start)
                if p_start is None:
                    return
                y0 = np.log(np.maximum(p_start, 1e-300))
                y0 -= float(np.mean(y0))
                y0 = np.clip(y0, -30.0, 30.0)

                end_t = min(deadline - 3.0, time.monotonic() + seconds)
                cmax_ref = float(np.max(conv_self(p_start)))
                if cmax_ref <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax_ref):
                    cmax_ref = 1.0 / n
                T = float(T_factor) / cmax_ref
                obj_scale = float(n)
                ridge = 1e-8

                def fun(y):
                    if time.monotonic() > end_t:
                        raise _Timeout()
                    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
                    if y.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
                        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    p = _softmax(y)
                    if p is None:
                        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    C = conv_self(p)
                    cmax = float(np.max(C))
                    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
                        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
                    if sc < best[1]:
                        best[1] = sc
                        best[0] = p.copy()

                    z = T * (C - cmax)
                    with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore"):
                        ex = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
                    Z = float(np.sum(ex))
                    if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 1e-300:
                        q = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / (2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
                        M = cmax
                    else:
                        q = ex / Z
                        M = cmax + math.log(Z) / T

                    val = float(2.0 * n * M)
                    d = grad_quadratic(p, q)
                    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
                        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    mu = float(np.dot(p, d))
                    grad_y = float(2.0 * n) * p * (d - mu)
                    yc = y - float(np.mean(y))
                    val += ridge * float(np.dot(yc, yc))
                    grad_y += 2.0 * ridge * yc
                    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_y)):
                        grad_y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    return obj_scale * val, obj_scale * grad_y

                def cb(x):
                    return time.monotonic() >= end_t

                try:
                    _scipy_minimize(
                        fun, y0, jac=True,
                        method="L-BFGS-B",
                        bounds=bounds,
                        callback=cb,
                        options={
                            "maxiter": 350,
                            "maxfun": 3000,
                            "maxls": 20,
                            "maxcorr": 60,
                            "pgtol": 1e-14,
                            "ftol": 1e-12,
                            "disp": False,
                        }
                    )
                except _Timeout:
                    pass
                except Exception:
                    pass

            for tf, frac in ((20.0, 0.55), (40.0, 0.45)):
                if not time_ok(30.0):
                    break
                run_lbgfgs(best[0].copy(), frac * t_lbgfgs, tf)

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Exact subgradient / line-search polishing in log-simplex.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        def polish_active(p_start, seconds, t_factors):
            p = normalized_p(p_start)
            if p is None:
                return
            start_t = time.monotonic()
            deltas = np.array(
                [0.0001, 0.0003, 0.001, 0.003, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 100.0],
                dtype=np.float64
            )

            for tf in t_factors:
                improved = True
                while improved and (time.monotonic() - start_t) < seconds and time_ok(5.0):
                    C = conv_self(p)
                    M = float(np.max(C))
                    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
                        break
                    sc = float(2.0 * n * M)
                    if sc < best[1]:
                        best[1] = sc
                        best[0] = p.copy()

                    T = float(tf) / M
                    z = T * (C - M)
                    with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore"):
                        ex = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
                    Z = float(np.sum(ex))
                    if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 1e-300:
                        q = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / (2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
                    else:
                        q = ex / Z

                    d = grad_quadratic(p, q)
                    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
                        improved = False
                        break
                    h = d - float(np.dot(p, d))
                    maxh = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
                    if not np.isfinite(maxh) or maxh <= 1e-18:
                        improved = False
                        break

                    y0 = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                    y0 -= float(np.mean(y0))
                    base_scale = 1.0 / maxh
                    local_best = (sc, p.copy())
                    local_improved = False

                    for delta in deltas:
                        if (time.monotonic() - start_t) > seconds or not time_ok(2.0):
                            break
                        yn = y0 - (delta * base_scale) * h
                        pn = _softmax(yn)
                        if pn is None:
                            continue
                        Cn = conv_self(pn)
                        scn = float(2.0 * n * np.max(Cn))
                        if np.isfinite(scn) and scn < local_best[0] - 1e-14:
                            local_best = (scn, pn.copy())
                            local_improved = True

                    if local_improved:
                        p = local_best[1]
                        if p is not None and local_best[0] < best[1]:
                            best[0] = best[0].copy() if hasattr(best[0], "copy") else p.copy()
                            best[1] = local_best[0]
                            best[0] = p.copy()
                        improved = local_improved
                    else:
                        improved = False

            # Final mirror sym probe.
            qs = 0.5 * (p + p[::-1])
            C = conv_self(qs)
            update_best_from_p(qs, C)

        if time_ok(20.0):
            polish_active(best[0].copy(), max(15.0, t_polish),
                          t_factors=(8.0, 24.0, 64.0))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final exact validation using the authoritative evaluator.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    candidate_arrays = [
        best[0],
        p0,
        np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n),
        0.5 * (best[0] + best[0][::-1]),
    ]

    # A couple of safe parent variants if time permits.
    if time_ok(8.0):
        cand = p0 + 1e-3 * rng.random(n)
        candidate_arrays.append(cand)
        cand = 0.5 * (p0 + p0[::-1]) + 1e-3 * rng.random(n)
        candidate_arrays.append(cand)

    def safe_exact(lst):
        try:
            val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
            if np.isfinite(val):
                return float(val)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return float("inf")

    final_best_list = None
    final_best_val = float("inf")

    for arr in candidate_arrays:
        if not time_ok(10.0):
            break
        p = normalized_p(arr)
        if p is None:
            continue
        # Return probability-scale sequence; evaluator is scale-invariant.
        out = [float(x) for x in p]
        if not out or any((not np.isfinite(x)) for x in out):
            continue
        val = safe_exact(out)
        if val < final_best_val:
            final_best_val = val
            final_best_list = out

    if final_best_list is None:
        final_best_list = [1.0 / float(n)] * n
        val = safe_exact(final_best_list)
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            final_best_list = [1.0]

    return final_best_list
```
